--- entity_google_reviews [row 3 of 5] ---
review_id: 7318
entity_slug: gts-on-the-bay
google_review_name: places/ChIJL5w8u7QHmogRiY0gzcejWpM/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT2tabWFsWXlkbDlYVUV4elZtTnljMmgwYVRVd1ZVRRAB
rating: 5
review_text: We always seek out a new to us place for dining on our trips to Gulf Shores, and saw lots of suggestions for GTs on The Bay!

Our visit started a bit awkward as we approached the hostess stand, none of the three ladies seemed interested in greeting us. So we politely let them know we had a party of 4 and one of them made the sacrifice of leading us to a table.

Our waitress was very friendly and suggested some appetizers from the happy hour menu despite it being almost an hour past happy hour.  We started with the fried pickles and cheese pizza.  The fried pickles had a delicate coating of batter and a clean fry, and nice pickle flavor.  Instead of a pizza they brought out chips and queso, which got returned and a fabulous cheese pizza was brought out rather quickly much to the delight of our kids.  The pizza was better than most take out pizza chains and a better price. Ours turned out to be free as the waitress and server had a discussion at our table about not being able to get us the happy hour pricing on the appetizers and we paid full price on the pickles.

For entrees we tried the two pasta dishes, chicken strips, and cheeseburger. These were all cooked and seasoned to perfection and we all loved the food!

We will definitely be coming back regularly to GTs, the food is that good!

--- entity_reviews [row 3 of 5] ---
id: 77e933da-e745-47ef-9072-6ad7b3f4b20b
entity_slug: gts-on-the-bay
reviewer_name: Jason Allen
rating: 5
body: We always seek out a new to us place for dining on our trips to Gulf Shores, and saw lots of suggestions for GTs on The Bay!

Our visit started a bit awkward as we approached the hostess stand, none of the three ladies seemed interested in greeting us. So we politely let them know we had a party of 4 and one of them made the sacrifice of leading us to a table.

Our waitress was very friendly and suggested some appetizers from the happy hour menu despite it being almost an hour past happy hour.  We started with the fried pickles and cheese pizza.  The fried pickles had a delicate coating of batter and a clean fry, and nice pickle flavor.  Instead of a pizza they brought out chips and queso, which got returned and a fabulous cheese pizza was brought out rather quickly much to the delight of our kids.  The pizza was better than most take out pizza chains and a better price. Ours turned out to be free as the waitress and server had a discussion at our table about not being able to get us the happy hour pricing on the appetizers and we paid full price on the pickles.

For entrees we tried the two pasta dishes, chicken strips, and cheeseburger. These were all cooked and seasoned to perfection and we all loved the food!

We will definitely be coming back regularly to GTs, the food is that good!
